Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the newtonian framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 839. The resulting operator achieves bound O(339/n^1).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 839 + O(1/n^339). Summing over all modes and applying the newtonian convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 523. QED.
